# Project brief — تطبيق نادي المستثمرين

Last updated: 2026-09-16 (owner's closing-scope decisions recorded in §5.5 and as M9 to M12: «رسائل الإدارة» first, neutral first with «دليل المحايد» and workshops, «شخصية ومسيرة» self-application; members' chat cancelled the same day; the dashboard gets its own domain; M7 part 2 B is open as PR #5 on branch `m7-payments`, waiting for the store accounts, Firebase and RevenueCat setup and the owner's OK for ONE preview build). These are the owner's decisions. If anything here conflicts with what the owner says in a session, ask before acting.

## 1. Entities and contacts
- Publisher and membership seller: شركة المجتمع الافتراضي للاستثمار, the official operator of نادي المستثمرين (vcmem.com). CR 2050179051, VAT 311933896300003, Riyadh, Al Olaya.
- Parent: Vibes Ventures — legal name VIBES VENTURES COMPANY HOLDING (renamed 2026-10-15; formerly شركة ذبذبات للاستثمار القابضة / فايبز القابضة). The brand is ALWAYS written in Latin «Vibes Ventures», even inside Arabic copy (owner, 2026-10-15: the Arabic transliteration looks bad). Owner of the «نادي المستثمرين®» trademark. Founder: م. سالم المسرحي.
- Management (the only management number — owner, 2026-10-17, URGENT correction): **+966 55 162 3111** (the official fixed management number). The numbers +966 55 831 8777 and +966 50 384 4438 are the owner's PERSONAL numbers and must never be published as the management contact; they also must not appear as phone-format examples or placeholders (use 0512345678).
- Studio bookings (بودكاست الملتقى): +966 53 846 1110.
- Club size: 11,000+ members. Target 20,000, then 100,000 after the app launch.

## 2. Architecture
The app calls only `server/`. The server talks to:
- **vcmem.com hub** (WordPress, Vibes AI Assistant plugin in hub mode): accounts, e-mail OTP, memberships, AI chat (n8n workflow "Vibes Web Agent"), staff console, admin roles. REST namespace `vibes-ai/v1`.
- **vibesholding.com Projects Bank** (plugin `projects-directory-pro`): projects feed and unlocks.
- **Paymob KSA**: real-world services only, Intention API, per-payment notification URL pointing to the server.
- **App stores via RevenueCat** (later): membership purchase → server → hub activation.
- **News sources + OpenAI API** (later): classification, deduplication and ranking only.

### 2.1 Hub bridge (later milestone)
The hub needs server-to-server endpoints secured by a bridge key. They are added to the Vibes AI Assistant plugin in a Claude chat session where the owner uploads the current plugin zip. Never guess the plugin's code.
Needed: register with OTP, verify/resend OTP, login returning a token for the server, profile get/update/avatar, delete account, membership status (unactivated, or annual with days left, AI «رصيد», project unlocks left), activate membership from a store purchase, chat send (text/voice/image) and history, staff-reply webhook to the server (for push), admin check.

### 2.2 Projects Bank feed
- `GET https://vibesholding.com/wp-json/pb/v1/projects?key=…` (plus `/ping`). The key lives in server env only.
- Post type `project`. Meta: project_number, company_name, founder_name, whatsapp, email, website, pitch_deck, project_gallery, project_details (Arabic body; post_content is empty), project_details_en, excerpt_en, title_en, company_name_en, founder_name_en, is_featured (golden flag), featured_order, golden_partner_url, views_count. Taxonomies: `sector`, `project_stage`.
- whatsapp, email, website and pitch_deck are private (CLAUDE.md rule 4).
- Unlocks live in table `wp_pdp_unlocks`, keyed by the hub contact id. A paid member gets 5 project unlocks per membership year. Golden projects never deduct credit and route to golden_partner_url.
- A server-to-server unlock endpoint is added to the Projects Bank plugin in a later milestone (the owner uploads the plugin zip in a Claude chat).
- Sort options used on the web: الأحدث / الأكثر مشاهدة / اكتشف / المشاريع الذهبية.

## 3. Accounts (mirror the hub; the hub enforces them)
- E-mail (owner decision 2026-10-13): any valid address, personal or company, verified by e-mail OTP. (Was: Gmail/iCloud/Outlook/`.sa` only.)
- Phone (owner decision 2026-10-13, hub 2.16.0): registration is open worldwide — all 198 countries selectable (GCC first with strict number rules and examples, then Arab countries, then the rest by Arabic name), Saudi by default, format `0512345678` for Saudi (no spaces, no country code; never a real personal number as the example — owner, 2026-10-17). Non-GCC numbers pass a generic length rule (5–12 digits after the dial code, E.164 ≤ 15). Notice text: «التسجيل متاح من أي دولة في العالم». The canonical list lives in the hub plugin (`vai_gcc_countries`) and is mirrored in `server/src/hub/countries.ts` (generated by `scratchpad gen-countries.mjs`, kept in sync by hand).
- Mandatory persona: «رائد أعمال — لدي مشروع مميز» / «مستثمر — أبحث عن فرص شراكة واعدة» / «محايد — شريك المستقبل وسأحدد توجهي لاحقًا». Mandatory short bio. Optional job title, photo, social links, company website.
- States: guest → registered but unpaid «عضوية غير مفعّلة» (message «برجاء تفعيل عضويتك») → annual member (360-day countdown) → admin (promoted in the hub).
- Guests may browse public content: project list and project pages without private fields, the golden portal, and Saudi decisions news.
- In-app account deletion is required by both stores.

## 4. Annual membership (single tier)
- 1,900 SAR per year ($665), promotional «لفترة محدودة», base price 3,900 SAR ($1,040). The in-app store price point is decided later.
- NEW DIRECTION (owner's vcmem membership page, 2026-10-14): worldwide membership «من السعودية إلى العالم» — registration from any country, events held at the Riyadh HQ and streamed online to members everywhere; the «لم تحدّد وجهتك بعد؟» neutral-first framing. HQ facts: العليا، شارع الأمير محمد بن عبدالعزيز — 400 m², 15 offices, stage + theater seating 80, meeting room (8 people, Zoom), podcast studio, business incubator; Projects Bank > 260 projects.
- Benefits (server-driven content; FACTS CHANGED 2026-10-14 — the old values in parentheses must never come back):
  - Project assessed within 10 WORKING DAYS, then listed in the Projects Bank with priority (was «خلال 3 أيام كحد أقصى»).
  - Member's project re-highlighted ONCE A MONTH to all members (was «3 مرات شهريًا»); «شخصية ومسيرة» feature (vcmem.com/famous).
  - Projects Bank credit worth 2,500 SAR ($667) = full details of 5 projects.
  - AI advisor with a daily renewing «رصيد»; the online consultation is with the SMART ADVISOR «مستشار ذكي مدعوم بالذكاء الاصطناعي» (was «خبراء النادي» — that concept is gone).
  - Riyadh HQ access by booking; club theater (80 seats — was 60) and meeting room (8 people, Zoom) at 50%.
  - 50% off: اصنع ملتقاك، Pitch Deck (عبر شركة PV)، بودكاست الملتقى studio packages (no usage limit), Momentum website/app design, e-marketing packages, influencer ads.
  - «شركاؤنا» partners program (M68): every subscribed member carries ONE permanent personal promo code (5 letters/digits, first letter = category N/E/I) honoured by EVERY partner; shown in the app (partners screen + membership card); partners managed entirely from the dashboard (logo, name, description, discount line, buy link, order, visibility); the dashboard looks any code up to tell the owner whose it is.
  - Coming soon is now a GENERIC line («جارٍ ربط جهات عديدة…») — the old named list (وديني القابضة، سكة، وديني سكاي، PV spaces) left the page.

### 4.1 Membership benefits page copy (owner, 2026-09-12) — SUPERSEDED 2026-10-14 by the new vcmem page (see §4 and server/src/content/membership.ts v5, which carries the new copy verbatim)
Groups and lines as the owner wrote them (server-driven; studio lines link to WhatsApp +966 53 846 1110):
- **بنك المشاريع**: رصيد 3,750 ريال في بنك المشاريع يعادل 5 مشاريع كاملة تختارها بنفسك · أولوية عرض مشروعك على المستثمرين في بنك المشاريع خلال 3 أيام كحد أقصى · نشر مشروعك على منصات النادي 3 مرات شهريًا على القروبات والمنصات والتطبيق · التقديم على شركاء النجاح بأولوية · تصفّح كل مشاريع بنك المشاريع · التواصل المباشر مع مؤسسي المشاريع.
- **حضورك وأدواتك**: أولوية الحضور في ملتقيات النادي وملتقى «5 دقائق» — حضوريًا في الرياض أو عبر الإنترنت · دخول مقر النادي بالرياض (باركود الدخول يُصدر للأعضاء المشتركين بعد حجز الموعد) · استشارة أونلاين مع خبراء النادي بدون رسوم · مساعد الذكاء الاصطناعي برصيد يومي متجدد على مدار الساعة · ظهورك في «شخصية ومسيرة» بدون رسوم · الانضمام إلى قروبات النادي · حساب واحد لكل مواقع المنظومة · حجز الاستديو (بودكاست الملتقى) للأعضاء المشتركين عبر واتساب الاستديو.
- **خصومات المنظومة**: خصم 50% على مسرح النادي وقاعة الاجتماعات · خصم 50% على خدمات المنظومة (اصنع ملتقاك، Pitch Deck، مومنتوم، بودكاست الملتقى، حقائب الامتياز) · كود خصم حتى 20% على منتجات «منفذ».
- **خصومات قادمة تُضاف لعضويتك تلقائيًا** (كلها «قريبًا»): خصومات شركة وديني القابضة (النقل والشحن والسفر في تطبيق واحد) · خصومات تطبيق سكة (النقل المدرسي التشاركي) · مزايا وديني سكاي (مقاعد الطيران الخاص) · أسعار خاصة في مساحات PV (مساحات ومكاتب العمل داخل المنظومة).
## 5. Version 1 features
1. **Home**: dashboard-driven banners and sections. Each banner has an audience (all / members / unactivated / persona) and start and end dates.
2. **Projects Bank**: search in Arabic and English, sector and stage filters, sort options, project page, unlock for paid members.
3. **Golden projects portal** («المشاريع الذهبية», the projects carrying علامة V): the umbrella plus 10 companies, portfolio value 161M SAR. Disclaimer: «المعلومات تعريفية وليست عرضًا تعاقديًا أو ضمانًا لعوائد».
4. **Membership screen**: benefits and status (store purchase in a later milestone).
5. **AI advisor**: the same hub conversation the member has on the websites; text, voice, image. «اسأل المستشار» sends the current screen's context (project or news item).
6. **Services**: list price and member price. Real-world services paid via Paymob. Studio booking: package and preferred time, handed to the studio WhatsApp. Project registration: شركاء النجاح (pvspaces.com/sp/) → specialised committee review → listed in بنك المشاريع only if approved.
7. **News**: fixed «قرارات وأنظمة المملكة» section for everyone; personalized feed by interests chosen at signup; sports is a full section of its own (owner, 2026-10-04, replacing the business-angle-only rule: «الناس تحب الأخبار الرياضية»; the decisions section still never carries sports). Source tiers: Saudi official → trusted Saudi media → global; أرقام is the primary source (boost 25), واس fully disabled (the owner may re-enable from the dashboard).
8. **HQ visit** (paid members only): date, time and purpose → admin confirmation → QR pass valid for that slot only. No walk-ins.
9. **Account**: profile, photo, digital membership card, delete account.

### 5.5 Owner decisions of 2026-09-16 (closing scope before launch, in the owner's priority order)
The owner's words: «عايزين نقفل كل شيء في التطبيق»; build after the M7 preview build, in this order, then M8 (launch).
1. **«رسائل الإدارة» first** (owner: «دي تكون أول حاجة، رسايل الأدمن مهمة جدًا»): a dashboard section where the admin publishes daily posts with text, links, images and video; the posts are the first block of the app (top of the home), each post can send a push notification. This is the «أخبار النادي» content of the 2026-09-11 decision, now the dashboard's first section.
2. **Neutral first, everywhere** («محايد أول شيء وبعدين رائد الأعمال وبعدين المستثمر، ده في كل شيء»): the persona order becomes محايد → رائد أعمال → مستثمر in the home portals, the registration persona choice, the advisor opening and every list or filter.
3. **The neutral is number one** («اهتم أوي بالمحايد… المحايد عندي رقم 1»): a dedicated page «دليل المحايد» that tells the neutral what to do and where to go (steps with in-app calls to action: browse the Projects Bank, the advisor, the workshops, the membership); neutrals browse the projects (public fields) and benefit from the workshops all year: an in-app workshops page with the schedule and registration (today workshops are a web link in §5.2).
4. **«شخصية ومسيرة» in the app** (the owner also wrote «سيرة ومسيرة»: confirm the section title before building): a section at the beginning of the app with the owner's line «لديك الخبرات والمهارات والمؤهلات والإمكانات التي تؤهلك لتكون إحدى شخصيات …» (the owner completes the sentence); a member applies from the app (form: bio, career milestones, photo, links), gets access to their profile page after the admin approves it in the dashboard; the admin can also enter and edit profiles there (extends the 2026-09-11 decision).
5. **Members' chat**: cancelled by the owner on 2026-09-16 («كنسل فكرة الدردشة دلوقتي خالص»). Not built; no rooms, no voice notes, no direct messages.
6. **Golden offer pages in-app** (2026-09-13, §5.1) stays in the list.
**Dashboard hosting (2026-09-16)**: the owner reserved a dedicated domain that will serve the dashboard only (2026-09-17: `vibesholdingsa.com`, bought at Hostinger, DNS at Hostinger; planned host `dashboard.vibesholdingsa.com` by CNAME; whether the apex can point at Railway from Hostinger DNS is NOT verified, check the record Railway asks for when the domain is added). The dashboard is the `admin/` web app, served at that domain (Railway custom domain on the same project), signing in with hub admin accounts through the same API.
Also from the owner: nothing should require leaving the app; every web link in §5.2 gets an in-app page over time.

## 7. Environments
- **test** (now): Railway environment `test`; Paymob TEST secret and public keys with TEST integration IDs; preview builds only.
- The hub and the Projects Bank exist only as live systems. In test, the server reads them read-only. Never trigger a real membership activation, a real payment, or e-mails to non-admin users. Test login with existing admin accounts only; do not create registrations on the live hub without the owner's approval.
- **production**: only after the owner's explicit go-live.

The first two M3 OTA updates closed the app on the M1 APK: a lazy require() of expo-secure-store (missing from that binary) is reported as a fatal JS error before try/catch runs. Server: `GET /api/membership/store` (public RevenueCat SDK keys from `REVENUECAT_PUBLIC_KEY_ANDROID` / `REVENUECAT_PUBLIC_KEY_IOS`, `STORE_MEMBERSHIP_PRODUCT` default `club_membership_annual`, `REVENUECAT_ENTITLEMENT` default `membership`, `STORE_TERMS_URL` / `STORE_PRIVACY_URL`, app user id prefix `vc-`); `POST /api/membership/sync` (session): drops the cached account and, when `REVENUECAT_SECRET_KEY` is set, reads the subscriber from RevenueCat's REST API v1 and activates an active entitlement the webhook has not activated yet (recorded as a `SYNC` event); activation is idempotent (a repeated webhook event id answers from the record; a second activation for the same contact with the same expiry or transaction id is recorded as `duplicate`, so the hub is never asked twice for one purchase). Push: `server/src/push/` (`service.ts`: kv `push:tokens`, one entry per Expo token owned by the account that last signed in on that device, pruned after 120 days; sends through `https://exp.host/--/api/v2/push/send` in chunks of 100 with `channelId: default`, optional `EXPO_PUSH_ACCESS_TOKEN`; `DeviceNotRegistered` tickets drop the token; push receipts are not polled yet); Arabic member messages for the HQ decision (confirmed → `/hq/pass/<id>`, rejected → `/hq`), the payment result (`/payment/<id>`) and the membership activation, also the manual-activation case (`/membership`); routes `POST` / `DELETE /api/push/tokens` (session), `GET /api/admin/push/tokens` (counts), `POST /api/admin/push/test` (sends to the admin's own devices or to `contactId`); `/health` → `push` and `membership.store`; every variable is in `server/.env.example`. `m7b.test.ts` (store settings, token registry, HQ and payment pushes, webhook retry and duplicate guard, sync through a stubbed REST API): `npm test` 61 pass. App (`expo-notifications` ~57.0.18, `react-native-purchases` ^10.9.1): both modules are gated like expo-secure-store (`lib/purchases.ts` checks `NativeModules.RNPurchases`, `lib/notifications.ts` checks `ExpoPushTokenManager` + `ExpoNotificationsEmitter` before requiring), so the JS is OTA-safe on the current preview binary (there the membership screen says this version cannot buy from the store yet and no push token exists). `components/StorePurchaseCard` on `membership` (signed-in accounts): configures RevenueCat with the server keys and `vc-<id>`, shows the store price (`priceString`) with the auto-renewal text, the test-environment note and the terms/privacy links, «اشترك الآن» → store sheet → `POST /api/membership/sync` every 3 s up to 8 times → «تم تفعيل عضويتك» or the pending notice («سنرسل لك إشعارًا»), «استعادة المشتريات»; active members only get «إدارة الاشتراك من المتجر» when the store knows a management URL; sign-out calls `Purchases.logOut()`. `components/PushRegistrar` (root layout): after login asks permission, creates the Android channel `default`, registers the Expo token (`POST /api/push/tokens` with the EAS `projectId`), routes notification taps through `data.screen` (cold start included) and refreshes the account / payments / HQ queries behind foreground arrivals; sign-out unregisters the token before clearing the session. `app.config.ts`: the `expo-notifications` plugin (white club mark `assets/images/notification-icon.png` generated from the logo, gold accent, channel `default`) and `android.googleServicesFile` from the `GOOGLE_SERVICES_JSON` variable (an EAS file variable on builds, git-ignored locally; nothing when unset).

One bug found and fixed on the way: `setNotificationChannelAsync` refuses `sound: 'default'` (expo-notifications treats it as a custom sound file) and that error had aborted the permission step. The store price and the purchase sheet themselves need a real RevenueCat key and a Play build: Play billing does not exist on the emulator. Server `server/src/media/`: `POST /api/admin/uploads` (admin) answers an upload ticket: with a bucket a presigned POST whose policy pins key, content type and size (the browser sends the file straight to the bucket), without one a signed `PUT /api/admin/uploads/put?ticket=` to the local disk store `UPLOADS_DIR` (development and tests only, `durable: false`, refused in production); `GET /api/admin/uploads/config`; public `GET /media/<key>` = 302 to a one-hour presigned link (bucket) or the file with ranges (disk); keys are server-made `posts/YYYY/MM/<uuid>.<ext>`; images JPG/PNG/WebP/GIF up to `UPLOAD_MAX_IMAGE_MB` (10), video MP4/MOV/WebM up to `UPLOAD_MAX_VIDEO_MB` (200), QuickTime is stored and served as MP4 (Android's browser refuses the QuickTime label), SVG/HTML refused. Posts: input `videoFile { url, poster }`, output `video { url, poster } | null` (older posts have no key), `images` unchanged (uploaded images are `/media/` URLs next to pasted links); saving checks our media with HEAD (`invalid_media`), files a post stops using are removed unless another post uses them, an opt-in daily sweep (`UPLOADS_SWEEP=1` and Postgres) removes day-old abandoned uploads and never runs when no post references any file; the server writes the bucket CORS rules from `ADMIN_ORIGINS` at boot only on Railway (`/health` -> `uploads.cors`). Variables in `server/.env.example`: `S3_BUCKET`, `S3_ENDPOINT`, `S3_REGION`, `S3_ACCESS_KEY_ID`, `S3_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` (set them by reference to the Railway Bucket), `S3_FORCE_PATH_STYLE`, `UPLOADS_DIR`, `UPLOAD_MAX_*`, `UPLOADS_SWEEP`. New packages: `@aws-sdk/client-s3`, `@aws-sdk/s3-presigned-post`, `@aws-sdk/s3-request-presigner`. Dashboard: «إضافة صور» / «إضافة فيديو» pickers, progress with cancel, thumbnail grid with reorder and remove, poster frame captured in the browser, limits checked before sending, warning when files are not durable. App (JS only, OTA-safe): `post.video` tile on the post screen and the play mark / poster on the cards, opened through `openLink` (in-app browser). Verified: `npm test` 85 pass (13 in `src/m15.test.ts`: tickets, disk store, ranges, traversal, posts with media, clean-up, sweep, a presigned bucket ticket built offline), server / admin / app typecheck, admin build, app lint; in the browser against a local server (mock hub, disk store): two images and a WebM clip uploaded from the editor at 375px, SVG refused in Arabic, poster captured, publish, the public feed carries `video`, re-edit right after save keeps the files, two batches keep their picked order. A multi-agent review found and the code fixes: an unauthenticated large-body path on the PUT route, a sweep that trusted one server's view of the posts, deletion inside the size check, a stale list in the dashboard that could delete fresh uploads, interleaved image order. NOT verified: anything against a real bucket (presigned POST from a browser, CORS, the 302 for expo-image and for video in the in-app browser), the app tile on the emulator or a device, HEVC `.mov` from an iPhone on Android. Known leftovers: `npm audit` flags `nodemailer` (older finding, not from this change); reorder buttons lose keyboard focus after a move. - [ ] **M18 — Dashboard sign-in code by e-mail** (owner, 2026-09-20: «باسوورد يجي على إيميل يكون متاح لمدة دقيقة واحدة فقط وبعد كده يصبح غير صالح»): a one-time code e-mailed to the admin when signing in to the dashboard, valid 60 seconds (make it a variable, e.g. `ADMIN_OTP_SECONDS`), single use. Constraints: rule 2, the identity stays the hub's (the code is a step on top of the hub account, never a separate admin registry); store only a hash of the code, limit attempts and resends, never log the code; the app's member sign-in is not affected. Risk to tell the owner: mail delivery can take longer than a minute (measure the SMTP delay first, offer «إعادة الإرسال»). To confirm at the start: a second step after the hub password, or a code instead of the password. Owner's decision (2026-09-20): a second step after the hub password.
  - Status (2026-09-20, branch `m9-admin-posts`, NOT pushed, NOT deployed, `ADMIN_OTP` stays 0 until the mail delay is measured, so the box stays open). Server: `server/src/auth/adminOtp.ts` (challenge store: code hashed with the challenge token, 60 s life from `ADMIN_OTP_SECONDS`, single use, 5 wrong codes end the challenge, first mail + 3 resends with a 20 s gap, challenge lives 10 min; the 60 s start when the mail has left, and a resend whose mail fails changes nothing; per hub account, whatever the address: one live challenge, 6 challenges and 10 wrong codes an hour, then `otp_cooldown`; steps run one at a time per challenge and per account because the KV has no atomic counter); `AuthService.adminLogin / adminVerify / adminResend`; routes `POST /api/admin/auth/login | verify | resend` (per-address limits). The hub still checks the password and the admin role (rule 2), the role is read again at verify; the session record gets `adminVerifiedAt`, and `dashboardGuard` (posts, uploads, push, payments, membership purchases) asks for it while `ADMIN_OTP=1` and for a new code after `ADMIN_SESSION_HOURS` (12); a lapsed dashboard session is revoked on its first refused request. `/api/admin/hq/*` keeps `adminGuard`: the app's reception screens and member sign-in are untouched. The code is only in the mail body (never subject, log, response or storage). No SMTP on the live hub = the dashboard refuses to sign in (`otp_mail_off`, fail closed); way back in: set `ADMIN_OTP=0` on Railway. `/health` -> `hub.adminOtp`: `off | on | no_mail`. On the mock hub without SMTP the code is the mock's public test code (local runs). Dashboard: `Login.tsx` code step with a seconds countdown, «إعادة الإرسال» and «رجوع»; it signs in through `/api/admin/auth/login` in both modes and falls back to `/api/auth/login` only when the server answers 404 `not_found` (a server from before M18), so the dashboard and the API can deploy in any order; after the hub's own «pending» e-mail code it drops that member session and signs in again through the dashboard route. Verified: `npm test` 101 pass (16 in `src/m18.test.ts`), server and admin typecheck, admin build, and in the browser against a local server (mock hub, `ADMIN_OTP=1`): code step, wrong code, used challenge, resend, sign-in, posts load. A multi-agent review (12 agents) confirmed 8 findings; fixed: no per-account limit, resend state changed before the mail left, the code clock started before the send, lapsed sessions never revoked, the «pending» path ending in a refused session, no fallback for an older server, the lock-out note in `.env.example`. Known leftovers: a post editor open across the 12 h cut-off loses its unsaved draft on save (wanted: ask for the code in place without unmounting the editor, `App.tsx` + `PostEditor.tsx`); the per-address limits trust `request.ip` with `trustProxy: true` (check how Railway sets `X-Forwarded-For` before relying on them; the per-account budget does not depend on it); `/health` says `on` when SMTP is configured, not when mail really arrives. NOT verified: a real mail through the hosting SMTP and its delay. To turn on: owner pushes; on Railway set `ADMIN_OTP=1` (variable writes are the owner's), check `/health` -> `hub.adminOtp: 'on'`, sign in once and time the mail; if it takes longer than a minute raise `ADMIN_OTP_SECONDS` (e.g. 180). An unverified hub e-mail still goes through the hub's own code first, then signs in again for the dashboard code. M25 (publisher role) must reuse `dashboardGuard`.

- [x] **M93 — Videos kick the user out of the app** (owner, 2026-10-18: «لسه ابلكيشن بيخرجني برا لما بشغل اي فيديو» + «عايزه يفتح جوا ابليكشن مش براه مش عايز اي حد يخرج من ابليكشن نهائي»). NOT a native-module crash: **build #4 exists** (versionCode 4, built ~2026-10-04 — the APK in the owner's Downloads) and its dex CONTAINS ExpoVideo + RNCWebViewModule + ExpoWebBrowser, so the M61 gates pass. Emulator repro (repacked build #4 + current JS, guest mode, deep link to /watch): the watch screen opens in-app, no crash — but YouTube's embed shows **«Video player configuration error — Error 153»**: since 2025 YouTube rejects an embed URL loaded directly in a WebView with no Referer, and its only working button «Watch video on YouTube» throws the user out to the YouTube app. FIX (JS-only, this session): (a) `watch.tsx` loads the embed as an iframe inside an HTML page with `baseUrl: 'https://www.vcmem.com'` (real origin → Referer → no 153), and `onShouldStartLoadWithRequest` blocks intent:/vnd.youtube: schemes and youtube watch/channel pages so NOTHING can leave the app; (b) `player.ts` `playVideo` always opens the in-app watch screen — the external openLink fallback is gone; the external «المشاهدة على يوتيوب»/«فتح الفيديو في المتصفح» buttons are removed and `watch.unavailable` now tells old binaries to update; (c) verified on the emulator: the club film plays INSIDE the watch screen (Arabic subtitles visible), app process alive. tsc + eslint clean. OWNER: `eas update --channel preview`, and the phone must run **build #4** (the Downloads APK — zero quota; build #3 has no player modules and now shows the update message in-app instead of opening the browser). Emulator gotcha recorded: with network up at launch, expo-updates replaces the repacked embedded bundle with the LIVE preview OTA — clear app data and launch offline (svc wifi/data disable), then re-enable network before playing. CLOSED 2026-10-18: owner published the OTA (M93+M94), installed build #4 from the EAS artifact link on his phone, and after the full close-reopen-twice cycle confirmed videos play inside the app («اشتغل جوه الأبلكيشن بعد إعادة التشغيل»); the same flow (pristine build #4 + live OTA, guest → videos → card) was verified end-to-end on the emulator.

Apple: code + server `APPLE_APP_IDS` ready since M46; the button appears with the first iOS build (M7) — enable «Sign in with Apple» on the App ID then.

  **OWNER'S TESTFLIGHT FEEDBACK (2026-10-23 ~02:00, testing from his personal non-admin account; TestFlight rig: internal group «Club Testers» + his personal ASC Admin user yasser4.designer@icloud.com «Mohamed Yasser» — he praised the iOS design):**
  (1) **iOS renders LTR** («الكلام جي من الشمال لليمين») — the M27 syncDirection() restart apparently doesn't apply RTL on iOS; force-quit retest still pending. Investigate direction.ts on iOS (reloadAsync vs cold start; may need the one-restart guard rethought or forcesRTL for ar-primary).
  **ROOT CAUSE FOUND + PATCHED 2026-10-23 (owner confirmed still LTR on his English-language iPhone):** `expo-localization`'s iOS module, with `supportsRTL` alone, RESETS `forceRTL` from the DEVICE locale on every cold start (`LocalizationModule.swift: forceRTL(isRTLPreferredForCurrentLocale())`), wiping the JS-persisted direction before React loads — so an Arabic app on an English iPhone always renders LTR (Android never touches the persisted flag, which is why it behaves; an English-device Android user would NOT have hit this). FIX: `patch-package` added (app devDependency + `postinstall`), patch `app/patches/expo-localization+57.0.2.patch` makes iOS keep the persisted forceRTL exactly like Android; `src/i18n/direction.ts` then converges with its existing one-restart guard. NATIVE change → needs **iOS build 3** (replace build 2 on the 1.0 version page before Add for Review; take the store screenshots from build 3 so they're RTL-correct). The next Android binary picks the patch up too (no behavior change for Arabic-device users). **BUILD 3 SHIPPED SAME NIGHT (owner's OK):** `eas build` + `eas submit` succeeded (build 3, 1.1.0, Apple processed it in minutes), build 2 DETACHED from the 1.0 version page and build 3 ATTACHED + saved (Claude in the pane; ASC's delete-build dialog renders blank on the first click — Escape + second click works). **✅ OWNER CONFIRMED ON HIS IPHONE («أيوة العربي بقى يمين-لشمال»): build 3 renders Arabic RTL — the M120 item (1) is CLOSED.** Remaining: 6.7" ar screenshots from build 3 + the subscription review screenshot (after the Paid Apps signature).

- [x] **M136 — TWO STORIES PER TOPIC PER DAY, FEED + PUSHES BY INTEREST (session 2026-10-28, owner: «عملاء كتير اشتكت من كتر الأخبار — خبرين فقط في اليوم لكل مجال، حسب اهتماماته، مش أكتر» → replaces the M135 hourly pushes; owner chose «حسب الاهتمامات» for the pushes too).** news/service.ts: the day has two EDITIONS at Riyadh 10:00 and 19:00 (`EDITION_HOURS`), filled on the first news poll after each hour (also on server start): each edition picks ONE story per topic — the best fresh story (last 24h, `scoreOf` + source boost) never picked before, each story placed under its most specific topic (the one fewest fresh stories carry, so «الاقتصاد» never swallows a startup/sports story) — so a topic never gets more than two stories a day. State in kv `news:daily:picks` (`{editions[] latest first (6 kept), pickedIds (600)}`); an edition with nothing fresh is not recorded and retries next poll. FEED (`GET /api/news/feed`) = the picks of the latest two editions only (before the first edition ever: a live preview of the same rule), narrowed to the member's interests (unified interests → legacy prefs → persona defaults; guests see every topic), a story shows under every interest it carries but no interest/topic chip shows more than two a day (`MAX_PER_TOPIC_PER_DAY`). PUSHES: each edition sends ONE push per device via NEW `PushService.broadcastEach` (per-account chooser, identical messages batched): the best pick among that account's news topics (resolver wired in app.ts: `news.useTopics` = interests.newsTopicsFor → news.prefs), else the edition's best story; per device language (en picks, ar fallback); title sports/top as before, body = source title (rule 7). So every device gets at most 2 news pushes a day (plus the inert M42 decision pushes, SPA disabled since M61). Admin send-now `POST /api/admin/news/push` unchanged (kv `push:news:daily` now only `{day, pushedIds}`). m134.digest.test.ts → m136.editions.test.ts (2 tests, the test day is tomorrow so the real clock never fills an edition); 330/330 pass, tsc clean. No app change, no OTA. DEPLOY: git push → Railway both. First edition after deploy fires on the first poll when Riyadh hour ≥ 10 (deploy at 15:00 → one push at once, next at 19:00).

CODE DONE (session 2026-10-28 evening): server 334/334 + tsc clean; admin tsc + vite build clean; app tsc + lint clean; the dashboard flow PROVEN in the browser pane against a local mock server (`.claude/launch.json` → `server-mock` = `node --env-file=server/.env.mock.local …tsx server/src/index.ts` on :3009, `admin` vite on :5173; `admin/.env.development.local` had a BOM that hid VITE_API_URL — stripped): password → enrolment page (QR + key + 3 steps) → first code → backup codes → session → «أمان الحساب» shows «مفعّل منذ 28 أكتوبر 2026، الأكواد الاحتياطية 8 من 8». SHIPPED 2026-10-28 ~17:00 UTC (owner clicks): pushed; `ADMIN_TOTP=1` + `ADMIN_OTP=1` on BOTH API services (/health of api + api-test: adminOtp=on, adminTotp=on); dashboard rebuilt (bundle index-B5nKSE47.js carries the enrolment); production OTA group fdae5bcd («M140 extra protection: authenticator app», runtime 1.1.0, ios+android, appEnv production — manifests verified on u.expo.dev). From now on every dashboard sign-in = password → e-mailed code → authenticator code; the first sign-in of each admin/moderator enrols (QR) and shows 8 backup codes once. Probe scripts on prod need the cached session (the e-mailed code cannot be answered) — `ADMIN_TOTP_SECRET` in .env.admin.local answers the authenticator step on TEST when ADMIN_OTP is off there. Design: RFC 6238 TOTP in `server/src/auth/totp.ts` (no new package: HMAC-SHA1 over node:crypto, base32 secrets, otpauth:// links with issuer «Investors Club», ±1 step window, replay guard on the last step, 8 hashed single-use backup codes); kv keys `totp:<contactId>`, `totp-setup:*`, `totp-step:*` (5-minute tokens, 5 attempts). MEMBERS (app, OTA): «إعدادات الأمان» → «حماية إضافية» card: enable = the server hands a secret + otpauth link (open the authenticator app directly or copy the key) → first code confirms → backup codes shown once; disable / new backup codes need a current code; sign-in (password, e-mail code, Google/Apple) then answers `totp: true` + stepToken and the app asks for the 6-digit code (`POST /api/auth/totp`). DASHBOARD: with `ADMIN_TOTP=1` every dashboard account (admin + moderator) must enrol: password → (e-mailed code when `ADMIN_OTP=1`) → authenticator code, or a QR enrolment on the first sign-in; a session without `totpVerifiedAt` is refused by dashboardGuard (403 `totp_required`); «أمان الحساب» section = status, re-link a new phone (current code or backup code), new backup codes; disabling is refused for dashboard accounts while the flag is on. Routes: `GET /api/me/totp`, `POST /api/me/totp/setup|confirm|backup|disable`, `POST /api/auth/totp`, `POST /api/admin/auth/totp/verify|enroll`. Railway (owner clicks, both servers): `ADMIN_OTP=1` (the M18 e-mailed code — the owner wants it on) + `ADMIN_TOTP=1`. Probe scripts: `admin-session.mjs` answers the authenticator step from `ADMIN_TOTP_SECRET` in .env.admin.local (the e-mailed code it cannot answer — prod probes then rely on the cached session).

- [x] **M142 — VOICE-FILLED FORMS: the member speaks, the advisor fills the whole form (M132(b), owner 2026-10-26: «بمجرد انه يسجل بس صوته يخليه يملي الفورم كامل بتاع البيانات»). DESIGNED then BUILT 2026-10-28 night (owner: «نفّذ M142»): server 338/338 + tsc clean, app tsc + lint clean, NOT yet pushed/deployed/OTA'd at write time.** SHIPPED AS DESIGNED: server `advisor/fill.ts` (FormFiller: chat completions, strict JSON schema built from the fields, temperature 0; phones → Saudi local / +intl via phone.ts, Arabic-Indic digits, e-mail/number/date/time validation, select + multiselect snapped to the real options with article-insensitive matching; known values survive silence; missing required → followUp question, model's or a fallback) + `POST /api/advisor/fill-form` (guests allowed for the registration form, per-IP limits 30/10 per 15 min) + `/api/advisor/transcribe` now accepts guests (8/15 min) — `m142.fill.test.ts` (4 tests). App: `components/VoiceFill.tsx` (gold «املأ بصوتك» pill, red pulsing while recording, hint line → «تمت التعبئة، راجع ثم أرسل» or the follow-up question; paper-aware; hidden when expo-audio is absent) wired into register (name/company/job/bio/interests by label), profile-edit, card delivery, HQ booking (kind/duration/purpose/note — day + slot stay a tap), شركاء النجاح application, service request panels (whatsapp + paymob), dynamic forms (FormEmbed, all but the consent box), posts/compose, workshops note; strings group `voiceFill` (ar + en), catalog 1134. ROLLOUT ORDER: owner pushes → Railway deploys BOTH servers (the route must exist first) → preview OTA → owner tests on his iPhone (mic already in the store binary) → production OTA. KEY FINDING: **NO new binary is needed.** The mic module `expo-audio` + the Arabic/EN microphone-permission text (M104 config plugin) were in package.json since 2026-10-19, and BOTH store binaries were built after that (Android production AAB vc 3 on 2026-10-22, iOS 1.1.0 build 3 on 2026-10-26, runtime 1.1.0) — so `VoiceButton.voiceInputAvailable()` is already true on every store install (quick zero-cost check: the mic shows in the advisor composer on the owner's iPhone). Everything below is JS + server → OTA on both channels. **Design:** (1) SERVER `POST /api/advisor/fill-form` (signed-in, rate-limited like transcribe): body `{ transcript, lang, fields: [{ key, label, type: text|phone|email|number|date|select|multiselect|textarea, options?, required? }], known?: {key: value} }` → OpenAI structured output (gpt-4o-mini, JSON schema built from the fields, temperature 0) returns `{ values: {key: value}, missing: [keys], followUp: "one short question in the member's language" }`; phone/e-mail/number normalised server-side (Arabic-Indic digits → ASCII, M126 phone rules, +966 defaults from the member's country), select values snapped to the closest option label or left missing; audio and transcripts are never stored or logged (M104 rule), reuse `advisor/voice.ts` for STT. (2) APP: one shared hook `useVoiceFill(fields, values, setValues)` + a gold «املأ بصوتك» pill (mic icon, recording pulse, «جارٍ التعبئة…») at the top of every form: tap → record (expo-audio, same tap-record/tap-stop as VoiceButton) → transcribe → fill-form → the fields animate in with their new values, filled fields get a light gold flash, missing required fields get the red «لم أسمع هذه» hint and the follow-up question is shown under the pill with a second mic tap that fills only the missing ones (`known` = what is already filled). The member ALWAYS reviews and presses the normal submit button himself — voice never submits. Guest forms get the same pill (register uses it for name/company/interests; phone and code stay typed). (3) FORMS COVERED (all use FormField today): register + profile-edit + persona (name, company, country, interests), card.tsx (delivery address: city, district, street, notes), hq/book (date, time, attendees, purpose), service/[key] request details, people/apply (شركاء النجاح founder application: project title, sector, stage, amount, description), workshops + agenda/[id] registration, form/[key] dynamic forms (FormEmbed builds `fields` from the server form definition), posts/compose (title + body), contact. Sign-in, codes, passwords, TOTP, payments and delete-account are EXCLUDED on purpose. (4) STRINGS through the catalog (ar + en, group `voiceFill`), `npm run strings`. (5) COST: STT < 3 halalas per minute of speech (M132(g)), extraction ≈ 1 halala per form (≈ 600 input tokens) → under 5 halalas per filled form; no new paid service. (6) TESTS: server unit test with a fake OpenAI (digits/phones/select snapping/missing list), app tsc + lint; verify on the emulator with a local server + on the owner's iPhone. ESTIMATE: one focused session (server endpoint + hook + wiring the ~12 forms), then preview OTA → owner test → production OTA.
- [x] **M145 — THE ADVISOR ACTS: HQ booking, screens and service requests from inside the chat (owner, 2026-10-28 night: «كل شئ تقدر تسويه بالمساعد الشخصي» → «نفذ»). CODE DONE same night (server 341/341 + tsc clean, app tsc + lint clean), NOT yet pushed/deployed/OTA'd at write time.** How it works: the app sends every chat line FIRST to `POST /api/advisor/intent` (session, 90/15 min per IP); `advisor/intents.ts` (IntentDetector, chat completions, strict JSON, temperature 0) reads it against the LIVE catalog (bookable HQ days, slot times, visit purposes, the whatsapp/paymob services with their fields, the app's screen list) and answers one of: **hq_book** (a proposal card — kind visit/office/meeting, hours, day, time, purpose, note; the server re-checks free slots per day via HqService.slots, lists what is still missing and returns chips: days, free times, purposes, kinds, hours; a chip pick calls the route again with text '' + the pending proposal = no model call; «تأكيد الحجز» calls the SAME `hqApi.book` the HQ screen uses, with the member's own session — the server never books by itself; success swaps the card for «تم إرسال طلب الحجز…» + a «حجوزاتي في المقر» button; a membership/profile gate shows the membership button), **screen** (one of 22 named screens → expo-router path in `components/advisor/screens.ts`), **service** (opens `/service/<key>?answers=…` with the spoken fields prefilled, choice fields snapped to the real options) or **none** → the line goes to the hub advisor exactly as before (an older server's 404 = none). Local exchanges (the member's line + the card) are ephemeral chat items (`addLocal`/`replaceLocal` in useAdvisorChat) — they are not stored on the hub, so they vanish on a full reload; the booking itself is on the server. App files: `components/advisor/HqProposalCard.tsx`, MessageBubble (3 new action types + `onAct`), AdvisorChat (intent-first submit, `act()`), api/advisor.ts types; strings `advisor.act.*` (ar + en, «Available times» — the catalog test forbids «free»). Tests `m145.intents.test.ts` (3). Ride-along: `snapOption` learned word-overlap matching with stop-words («جوله بالمقر» → «جولة في المقر»). NOT in v1 (next steps if the owner wants): services the agent submits itself (today it prefills and the member sends), card delivery / forms / profile edits from the chat, voice input inside the chat for these intents (the M104 mic already feeds the composer, so speaking «احجز لي القاعة بكرة 10» works end to end), and the hub-side n8n agent knowing about these app actions (the app answers them before the hub sees the line). ROLLOUT: same as M142 — push → Railway (both) → preview OTA → owner's iPhone → production OTA.

MEASURED FIRST (test API, 2026-10-29): the server is NOT the problem — every home request answers in 250–300 ms (home 7 KB, services 24 KB, videos 30 KB, projects 36 KB, posts 6 KB, meetups 7 KB, agenda/people/partners ≤ 2 KB, all gzip, all `no-store`), the ten of them in parallel finish in ~0.9 s; PB images are webp 50–100 KB with `max-age=43200`. The felt slowness is on the phone: (a) NO persisted query cache — every cold start waits for the network before the home draws anything (skeleton until `/api/home`, then the blocks mount and fire a SECOND wave: posts, agenda, people, projects, meetups ≈ +0.5 s); (b) staggered entrance delays up to 360 ms + per-card stagger make the page keep «arriving» after the data is there; (c) session restore (`/api/auth/me`) and the strings sync run at the same time as the home wave. PLAN (JS-only, ships by OTA): persist the TanStack cache on expo-file-system (already in the 1.1.0 binary) so the home paints instantly from the last visit and refreshes behind; prefetch the block queries in the first wave; cut the entrance delays; `useReducedMotion` + `GlowButton`/`useBreath` in `motion.tsx`; the mic and the send button in the advisor composer breathe with a soft gold halo (stronger while recording). WHAT CHANGED: (1) `app/src/lib/queryCache.ts` (new) — the public content queries (content, services, videos, partners, posts, people, agenda, projects, golden, meetups; never me/card/wallet) are dehydrated to `query-cache.v1.json` in the app sandbox (expo-file-system/legacy, coalesced to one write per second, 24 h max age) and hydrated behind the splash in `_layout.tsx`, so the home paints from the last visit and refreshes behind; (2) the home hoists `usePeople/useAgenda/useMeetupsContent/useProjectsList` so the blocks' requests join the first wave (before: skeleton → `/api/home` ≈ 0.45–0.9 s → blocks mount → second wave ≈ +0.5 s; after: one wave ≈ 0.9 s, and ~0 s from disk on a warm cache); (3) `motion.tsx`: `DELAY_SCALE = 0.55` on every FadeInView/PopIn delay, FadeInView 420→340 ms, `entranceDelay` cap 8→5 — the page stops «arriving» after the data is there; (4) `motion.tsx`: `useReducedMotion` (AccessibilityInfo reduceMotion, app-wide), `useBreath` (0→1→0 sine loop, native driver, held at 0.5 under reduce-motion) and `GlowButton` (breathing golden halo: ring scales 1.02→1.14 and brightens 0.3→1 over 2 s while the button scales 1→1.05; `strong` = neon ring 1.24, 1.3 s cycle); applied to the composer mic (`VoiceButton`, always breathing, strong while recording), the composer send (`Composer`, only while there is something to send — a wrapper around M151's version, nothing of M151 touched) and the M142 «املأ بصوتك» pill (`VoiceFill`); the advisor dock circle already breathes (M-earlier `usePulse`) and was left alone. NOT VERIFIED ON A DEVICE: the app has no web target (no react-native-web), so the browser pane cannot show it; the owner sees it on the store app after the OTA. OWNER TO RUN: `git push`, then `eas update --channel preview --environment preview --non-interactive --message "M152: faster home (persisted cache, one request wave, shorter entrances) + breathing mic/send"` inside `app/` with `NODE_OPTIONS=--use-system-ca`; production only after his OK. WHAT HE SHOULD SEE: the mic in the advisor bar pulses gold slowly all the time and turns into a fast red-neon pulse while recording; the send button breathes only when there is text or a file; the form mic pill breathes the same way; second open of the app shows the home immediately (no skeleton) and content refreshes quietly.
